# Polling and timed-wait inventory

Baseline: `d33ed17571`. Locations refer to that baseline so deletion does not erase the audit trail.

Scope: `rust/crates/**` and `typescript/packages/**`, excluding the separately owned `harness`, `harness-core`, `harness-tui` and `harness-client` packages. Product code, tests, conformance, examples, benchmarks and scripts are included.

Candidates include event-driven loops and retry identity references; these require classification, not mechanical removal. Pending rows are not approval to retain a timer. The only timing exceptions are caller-chosen request deadlines and backoff following transient transport failure. Tool/model retry and exact idempotent resend identities are allowed. Actor alarm implementations are the event primitive itself.

Real child status/invalid-deadline control added; existing capture/cleanup/deadline negatives retained, uncompiled/unrun | Actioned |

## Logical capacity audit

Audit basis: design audit v3 (2026-10-30), axis (f), and gap register G63â€“G67. This inventory distinguishes limits on complete logical work from pages, scheduling credit, physical protocol constraints and explicit caller capacity. Status starts as **open**; completion requires removal of the refusal/truncation and its hidden default, with consumer updates.

Excluded: `harness`, `harness-core`, `harness-tui`, `harness-client`, and `typescript/packages/harness` (tracked separately in #1744). MCP startup/tool defaults, agent depth/goals/checkpoints/children/history and the shell preset in those packages remain in that scope. The filesystem shell adapter must preserve the optional caller deadline supplied by that work.

| G63/G65, PLATFORM-7 | `stream/src/memory/memory_limits.rs`, `capacity.rs`, replay admission | 100k paths; 64 MiB path bytes; 1m records, commits and replay identities; 256 MiB payload | Actioned: six optional caller capacities default to None through admission, snapshots and WASM. Explicit small-capacity atomicity/replay/funding controls retained. Fixed-key namespace/replay indexing obligations remain pending. |
| G63/G65, PLATFORM-7 | `stream/src/local/local_stream_limits.rs`, local journal/recovery | 1m commands; 1 GiB journal | Actioned: removed journal admission/replay refusals; retained compaction_operations/compaction_bytes as maintenance triggers. Original append and exact replay survive reopen beyond a selected compaction target. Presence bits retain exact optional capacities in one header/snapshot format. |

Each call returns its original conditional result; rebase is an explicit caller operation. Exact ambiguous-commit identity/evidence remains fenced and recoverable. Original native custody and real conflict/idempotency/fence controls retained; uncompiled/unrun. |

| G63/G64 | Native-mount qualification subprocess output | Four/eight-MiB captured-output totals | Actioned: deleted both totals, temporary output files and repeated output-metadata admission; original requested pipe bytes and native physical cleanup own the result. Fixed child/tool deadlines are deleted; unrelated watch visibility waits remain Pending. Real process controls are uncompiled/unrun. |

Allowed bounds retained after inspection:

- Directory, result, event and output range page sizes split work and expose continuation.
- Active runtime slots, in-flight requests and byte credit delay admission without discarding logical intent.
- Cache capacity evicts reproducible data without limiting logical history.
- OS-enforced component/argv constraints apply only in the corresponding native adapter.
- Explicit caller reservations, capacities and deadlines remain explicit; absence must remain unbounded.
- Protective framing/decompression bounds need a concrete physical encoding or bomb-protection contract; a small logical envelope is not protection.

Validation obligations for each changed mechanism: preserve atomicity and authority; waits name their actual producer and report producer loss; admission/check overhead is constant in unrelated history/population. Negative controls restore a former cap, suppress/drop the producer, or grow unrelated history. Full requested output necessarily costs its own bytes/items; this is not a population scan. Record required syscalls, durability barriers, round trips, allocations and copies in the PR, including before/after counts where known.

## Rejecting source rule

Pending: `scripts/check-no-polling.mjs <baseline-revision>` compares real Git objects with tracked and untracked sources in the working tree. `scripts/no-polling.mjs` rejects new timer/readiness references, aliases, embedded timer programs and unclassified loops. JavaScript/TypeScript uses the existing TypeScript parser; native sources retain raw-string and nested-comment boundaries. Each disposition names one exact source fingerprint, classification, producer, terminal and negative control. Changing executable tokens reopens dispositions in that source unit; an accepted occurrence cannot authorize another copy.

The source controls cover direct timers, imported and assigned aliases, wrapped timer values, computed property concatenation, dynamic calls, template substitutions, regex boundaries, escaped identifiers, rechecks, child-status probes, timed condition waits, actual Git baselines and untracked sources. They are not yet executed. Required-check integration, exact disposition review and cross-file helper coverage remain pending; this source checkpoint does not close the static-rule obligation. Run the contributor command after installing dependencies: `bun run check:no-polling origin/main`.
